### X23 · low · bug · nginx sends two Content-Type headers on /health, and its automatic redirects drop the published port
- **Status:** CONFIRMED — record: `verdicts/v-infra.md`
- **Area / module / feature:** Infra / frontend/nginx.conf / Docker web server (port 3080): health endpoint, `/api` and `/assets` redirects
- **Location:** `frontend/nginx.conf:19` (+ `frontend/nginx.conf:1-5`, `frontend/nginx.conf:21-29`, `frontend/nginx.conf:34-37`, `frontend/nginx.conf:44-47`, `compose.yaml:45`, `docs/api.md:3`, `docs/openapi.yaml:14`)
- **Problem:** `location = /health { … return 200 'ok'; add_header Content-Type text/plain; }` sends two Content-Type headers, because `add_header` adds a second header next to the `default_type` one that `return` already set. Separately, nginx's automatic absolute redirects (the trailing-slash redirect for the `/api/` proxy location, and the directory redirect that `try_files $uri $uri/` issues for `/assets`) use the container's port 80, so the published port is dropped.
- **Trigger → consequence:** /health returns `Content-Type: application/octet-stream` followed by `Content-Type: text/plain`; a health client or load balancer that reads the first value sees octet-stream, and fetch combines them as "application/octet-stream, text/plain" (the Compose check `wget -q --spider`, compose.yaml:45, ignores the type, so nothing breaks today). Opening the documented workspace base URL `http://localhost:3080/api` (docs/api.md:3, docs/openapi.yaml:14) in a browser gives `301 Location: http://localhost/api/` on port 80: connection refused, or another local service. `/assets` redirects to `http://localhost/assets/`.
- **Evidence:** `curl -D - http://localhost:3191/health` → "HTTP/1.1 200 OK … Content-Type: application/octet-stream … Content-Type: text/plain", body "ok"; `curl -D - http://localhost:3191/api` → "301 Moved Permanently / Location: http://localhost/api/"; `/assets` → "Location: http://localhost/assets/"; with `Host: example.test:3191` → "Location: http://example.test/assets/", so the port is lost even when the client sends it.
- **Suggested fix:** In frontend/nginx.conf: `location = /health { access_log off; default_type text/plain; return 200 'ok'; }` (drop the `add_header`), and `absolute_redirect off;` at server level, so nginx writes relative `Location` headers (`/api/`, `/assets/`) that keep whatever host and port the browser used. List both under "Web server (port 3080)" in docs/api.md "Behavior changes".
- **Regression test:** Web-server behavior must be checked against the built image, not the Vite dev server (review-lessons.md:124), and no automated check exists today. Add `scripts/web_smoke.py` (stdlib urllib, `ARC_WEB_URL`, default `http://localhost:3080`, redirects not followed) and run it in CI after the Compose stack starts, next to smoke.py/studio_smoke.py: GET /health → 200 with exactly one Content-Type header, `text/plain`; GET /api and GET /assets → 301 whose Location is relative (`/api/`, `/assets/`) or keeps the request's host:port. Optionally fold the existing manual curl checks (cache headers, 404 for a missing asset) into the same script.
- **Related:** docs/review-lessons.md:124 (web-server checks against the built image); X17 (nginx HTML 413), X18 (refuted Host header claim).

## Frontend: rule deletion and the graph editor

<a id="x41"></a>

### X41 · high · bug · A stale saved copy of a deleted rule opens instead of the re-created rule, and Save overwrites the new rule
- **Status:** CONFIRMED — record: `verdicts/v-del-fe1.md`
- **Area / module / feature:** Frontend / app shell (`App.tsx` rule-session selection) / reopening a rule after rule deletion (saved copy versus detail read)
- **Location:** `frontend/src/App.tsx:78` (+ `frontend/src/App.tsx:82`, `frontend/src/App.tsx:95`, `frontend/src/types.ts:63`, `backend/src/main/java/dev/arc/rule/RuleService.java:177`, `backend/src/main/resources/db/migration/V1__rules.sql:7`)
- **Problem:** App keeps the last saved or created rule (`savedRule`, set by `acknowledgeSave`) and prefers it over the fresh detail read whenever `savedRule.id === ruleId && savedRule.revision >= detail.data.revision`. Since rule deletion (b8aa4ff) an ID can be deleted and created again, and the new rule restarts at revision 1, so ID + revision no longer identifies one rule. `forgetDeletedRule` (App.tsx:95-99) clears the copy only for deletions made in this tab, and the server's `revision(...)` check compares only the number, so a stale copy at an equal revision passes it.
- **Trigger → consequence:** Tab A creates or saves rule X, and `acknowledgeSave` keeps X at revision r (creation alone gives r = 1). Another tab, user or API client deletes X, creates a new X and brings it to revision r. Tab A opens X without reloading: the editor shows the deleted rule (heading "Old rule v-del-fe1", canvas `["Input…Inputs","Output…Old result…amount"]`). Tab A edits and clicks Save draft: the PUT carries revision 2 and returns 200, alerts are `["Draft saved"]`, and the server now holds `{"name":"Old rule v-del-fe1","description":"","kind":"RULE","revision":3,"inputs":["amount"],"nodes":["Inputs","Old result"]}` instead of the other client's rule (name "New rule by someone else", description "their work", 3 nodes, input country). The other client's name, description and draft are overwritten with no conflict; only the kind stays the new rule's. With a lower new revision, the tab still shows the deleted content and Save gets a 409.
- **Evidence:** App.tsx:76-80 `const selected = detail.data && (savedRule?.id === ruleId && savedRule.revision >= detail.data.revision ? savedRule : detail.data);`. RuleService.java `revision(...)` checks only `rule.revision() != revision`; V1__rules.sql:7 `revision INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1`. Browser probe on :3191 (spec deleted): "tab A saved old rule; server revision: 2", "other client's rule revision: 2 New rule by someone else RULE", "editor heading: Old rule v-del-fe1", "PUT status: 200 sent revision: 2". Not intended: docs/maintaining.md:131 covers only deletions made in this tab ("App forgets the rule's saved copy…"), and docs/api.md:97 says "the ID can name a new rule".
- **Suggested fix:** Identify rules by incarnation, not by ID. Add one shared helper, e.g. `frontend/src/domain/ruleIdentity.ts` with `sameRule(a, b)` (same `id` and same `createdAt`) and `ruleIncarnation(rule)` (e.g. `${id}@${createdAt}`); both `Rule` and `RuleSummary` carry `createdAt`, and `created_at DEFAULT now()` is new for a re-created row. At App.tsx:78 prefer `savedRule` only when `sameRule(savedRule, detail.data) && savedRule.revision >= detail.data.revision`; otherwise use `detail.data` and clear the copy. X01, X44 and X02 use the same helper. Residual on the server: an editor that is already open when another client deletes and re-creates the ID up to the same revision still passes the PUT/publish revision check. Decision needed: (a) make revisions unique across incarnations: a new Flyway migration adds a sequence (started above the current `MAX(revision)`) that feeds `rules.revision` on insert and replaces `revision = revision + 1` in `JdbcRuleRepository.update`/`publish`, so save, publish and X81's delete check reject a stale incarnation without new request fields; revisions then stop being consecutive per rule, which needs a docs/api.md "Behavior changes" entry. Or (b) send `createdAt` as an extra optional precondition on PUT and publish and answer 409 on a mismatch. Recommend (a): one server change protects every client and also makes `id:revision` keys (X01) unique. Refine lessons F5 and B26: an ID plus a counter is not a rule's identity once rules can be deleted.
- **Regression test:** frontend/tests/rule-session.spec.ts (reopened rule sessions): create X in the UI so App keeps its copy; through the API delete X, create a new X with another name and graph and save it up to the same revision; open X from the library and assert the heading and canvas show the new rule, and that after an edit plus Save draft the server keeps the new rule's name and description. If the choice is extracted as a pure function (e.g. `openedRule(savedRule, fetched)`), unit-test it in a new frontend/tests/unit spec. For option (a): backend `RuleServiceTest`/`JdbcRuleRepositoryTest` assert that a re-created rule's revision exceeds the deleted one's and that a PUT with the old revision returns 409; scripts/smoke.py checks the same over HTTP in its reused-ID scenario.
- **Related:** X01, X44, X02 (same root cause: an ID plus a counter used as permanent identity after b8aa4ff made IDs reusable); X81 (DELETE has no precondition at all); lessons F5, B26; duplicate X110 (registry).

<a id="x01"></a>

### X01 · low · bug · Library card previews of a re-created rule show the deleted rule's graph
- **Status:** CONFIRMED — record: `verdicts/v-del-fe1.md`
- **Area / module / feature:** Frontend / features/library (`previewCache`, `RuleCard`) / rule library card previews
- **Location:** `frontend/src/features/library/previewCache.ts:7` (+ `frontend/src/features/library/previewCache.ts:11`, `frontend/src/features/library/RuleCard.tsx:20`, `frontend/src/features/library/RuleCard.tsx:31`, `frontend/src/App.tsx:95`)
- **Problem:** Card previews are cached for the whole page session under `${rule.id}:${rule.revision}`, on the stated premise (previewCache.ts:11-12) "A saved draft gets a new revision, so a cached preview never outlives the draft it shows". A deleted rule's ID can be created again and its revision restarts at 1, so a new rule can hit the old entry. RuleCard skips its fetch on a hit (`enabled = !cached`), and nothing evicts an entry: not `forgetDeletedRule`, and not a deletion made elsewhere.
- **Trigger → consequence:** The library shows rule X at revision 1 and caches its preview. X is deleted, in the app or elsewhere, and created again with the same ID; in the app this happens naturally, because Create rule derives the ID from the name. The new X is at revision 1 with a different graph and kind. When the library page reloads (after a stale mark or a changed search), RuleCard hits the cache and never fetches: the card shows the new title ("New preview rv-v-del-fe1-pv-…") and footer ("Condition rule 3 nodes 1 input") above the old 2-node graph labelled "Current draft", and the preview's accessible name uses the old rule name. It lasts until the page is reloaded; opening the rule shows the right graph.
- **Evidence:** previewCache.ts:7-8 ``const previewKey = (rule) => `${rule.id}:${rule.revision}`;``; RuleCard.tsx:20 `const cached = cachedPreview(rule);` and :31 `!cached,`. Browser probe on :3191 (spec deleted): before `["Inputs","OLD result"]`, "recreated revision: 1"; after, title "New preview rv-v-del-fe1-pv-…", footer "Condition rule 3 nodes 1 input", preview aria-label "Old preview rv-v-del-fe1-pv-…: complete graph overview, 2 nodes, 1 connection" with labels `["Inputs","OLD result"]`, while the server draft labels are `["NEW inputs","NEW step","NEW result"]`.
- **Suggested fix:** Key previews by rule incarnation: build `previewKey` from the shared `ruleIncarnation(rule)` helper proposed in X41 plus the revision (e.g. `${id}@${createdAt}:${revision}`; `RuleSummary` already has `createdAt`, so widen the `Pick<RuleSummary, "id" | "revision">` parameters to include it), and add `createdAt` to RuleCard's `useAsyncResource` key (`${rule.id}:${rule.createdAt}:${rule.revision}:${attempt}`). Rewrite the comment at previewCache.ts:10-13 to name the incarnation. No eviction hook is needed, because a re-created rule never matches an old key. (X41 option (a) would also make `id:revision` unique; the explicit incarnation key stays correct either way.)
- **Regression test:** frontend/tests/unit/library-previews.spec.ts, next to "card previews are reused per rule revision and stay bounded": `cachedPreview` for the same `id` and `revision` but another `createdAt` returns undefined. Browser: frontend/tests/library-overview.spec.ts: create X, show the library, delete and re-create X with other nodes through the API, change the search so the page reloads, and assert the card preview's node labels are the new graph's.
- **Related:** X41, X44, X02 (same root cause); lesson F14 ("card previews are reused per rule ID and revision"; refine to the incarnation), B26; duplicates X43, X85, X112, X135 (registry).

<a id="x44"></a>

### X44 · medium · bug · The page-wide Formula metadata cache serves a deleted Formula's parameter contract after its ID is re-created
- **Status:** CONFIRMED — record: `verdicts/v-del-fe1.md`
- **Area / module / feature:** Frontend / features/studio (`formulaMetadata`, `useFormulaSupport`) / `@` Formula completion, hover and Published formulas insertion
- **Location:** `frontend/src/features/studio/formulaMetadata.ts:36` (+ `frontend/src/features/studio/formulaMetadata.ts:17`, `frontend/src/features/studio/formulaMetadata.ts:43`, `frontend/src/features/studio/formulaMetadata.ts:84`, `frontend/src/features/studio/useFormulaSupport.ts:210`, `frontend/src/features/studio/useFormulaSupport.ts:263`, `frontend/src/App.tsx:95`)
- **Problem:** The module-level singleton (`formulaMetadata`, line 84) treats an `id:version` pin as immutable (line 17) and keeps each pin's name and inputs for the page session. Deletion ends that immutability; docs/maintaining.md:38 ("Deleting a rule is the one end of a pin's immutability") handles it only on the backend (`Engine.forget`), and nothing evicts a frontend entry. The cached branch (34-41) returns before the `rule.kind !== "FORMULA"` check (43), and a catalog summary refreshes only the display name. Correction from verification: hover names are not frozen for good; hover alone (it passes no summary) never refreshes a name, but the next completion search or picker insertion rewrites the cached name.
- **Trigger → consequence:** `@tax` completion or hover caches `tax:1` with (amount: number, rate: number). `tax` is deleted, which is allowed because no stored draft calls it, then re-created with (country: string) and published as v1. In the same page: completion shows "New tax · @tax:1(amount: number, rate: number)", and hover shows the old contract (docs/studio.md:13 names hover as the way to see a call's parameter contract). Insertion writes the old argument list: the server rejects an arity mismatch at validation, but calls are positional (docs/api.md:29), so re-created same-typed inputs in a different order give a call that validates and passes values to the wrong parameters. If the ID is re-created as a non-Formula, hover still shows a Formula contract. Only a page reload fixes it.
- **Evidence:** formulaMetadata.ts:34-41 ``const key = `${id}:${version}`; const cached = this.entries.get(key); if (cached) { const current = summary ? { ...cached, name: summary.name } : cached; this.remember(key, current); return current; }`` runs before :43 `if (rule.kind !== "FORMULA") throw`. Unit probe (the real `FormulaMetadata` reading the live :8191 API; no probe file retained): "hover load after re-create: ["amount","rate"] Name FORMULA amount+rate" (server v1 inputs `["country"]`); search result `["Name FORMULA country",["amount","rate"]]`, while a fresh cache gives `["country"]`; "cached load of RULE kind: returned ["amount","rate"]" versus "fresh load of RULE kind: threw Only published Formula rules can be called in an expression.". Browser probe on :3191: "first suggestion: … Old tax · @…:1(amount: number, rate: number)"; after delete, re-create and publish, "second suggestion: … New tax · @…:1(amount: number, rate: number)"; after reload "(country: string)".
- **Suggested fix:** Make the rule incarnation part of the cached identity. Keep the `createdAt` of the rule each entry was read from (from the summary, or from `reads.rule`, which already returns the full `Rule`; widen `FormulaReads.rule`'s type to include `createdAt`). In `load`, when a `summary` is supplied and its `createdAt` differs from the entry's, drop the entry and take the uncached path, which re-runs the FORMULA kind check and reads the version again; this fixes completion and picker insertion. Add `forget(id)`, removing every `${id}:*` entry, and call it from `App.forgetDeletedRule` for same-tab deletions. Hover passes no summary. Decision needed: (a) hover keeps trusting the entry until a search, an insertion or a same-tab deletion replaces it (no extra requests, but another tab's re-creation shows the old contract on hover until the next `@` search), or (b) hover revalidates with one `reads.rule(id)` per hover (kind, name, `createdAt`) and reuses the cached version inputs only for the same incarnation. Recommend (b): hover is the documented place to read a call's contract, and each published version is still downloaded once per incarnation (lesson F31). Update the class comment (line 17) and docs/maintaining.md:38 to say that the frontend keys pins by incarnation.
- **Regression test:** frontend/tests/unit/formula-metadata.spec.ts (injected `FormulaReads`): (1) load `tax:1` with a summary (`createdAt` t1, inputs [amount, rate]), then search or load with a summary whose `createdAt` is t2 → `reads.version` is called again and the inputs are [country]; (2) with option (b), a load without a summary after re-creation as kind RULE throws "Only published Formula rules can be called in an expression."; (3) `forget("tax")` removes every cached version of `tax`. Browser: frontend/tests/formula-completion.spec.ts: after an API delete, re-create and publish of the same ID, the `@` suggestion detail and the hover show the new signature without a page reload.
- **Related:** X41, X01, X02 (same root cause); lesson F31 ("Cache immutable `id:version` metadata in one bounded page-wide cache"; refine to the incarnation), B26 (backend counterpart `Engine.forget`); duplicates X80, X111, X119 (registry).

<a id="x02"></a>

### X02 · low · bug · The API playground stays on a version that no longer exists after the selected rule is deleted and re-created elsewhere
- **Status:** CONFIRMED — record: `verdicts/v-del-fe1.md`
- **Area / module / feature:** Frontend / features/execution (`usePublishedExecution`) / API playground (published execution)
- **Location:** `frontend/src/features/execution/usePublishedExecution.ts:38` (+ `frontend/src/features/execution/usePublishedExecution.ts:81`, `frontend/src/features/execution/usePublishedExecution.ts:100`, `frontend/src/features/execution/usePublishedExecution.ts:167`, `frontend/src/app/useRuleLibrary.ts:28`)
- **Problem:** The playground's implicit version only ever moves upward, from three places: `selectionAfterCatalogPage` adopts a listed summary only if it is newer (38-40); `listedVersion` takes the max with App's hidden library page (81-84), which never reloads while hidden (useRuleLibrary.ts:28-32 reloads only `if (visible && stale)`); and `historyNewest` only grows (100). Lesson F5 ("must not move it backwards") predates deletion. After the selected rule is deleted, created again and republished as v1 elsewhere, the playground stays on a version that no longer exists, and Retry cannot recover. Without re-creation, the deleted rule stays listed through the `published` prepend (167-170) and stays executable.
- **Trigger → consequence:** The playground has X selected at v2. Another client deletes X, re-creates it and publishes v1. (a) A new search keeps Version at v2 with Execute enabled; Execute returns "Published rule version not found: X v2". (b) Choosing another rule and then X again still gives v2, because the hidden library page still lists X at v2; this time it is a load error with "Retry loading", and the cURL example contains `"version": 2`. (c) Retry loading recomputes the same v2. The stale library page also affects a freshly mounted playground. Recovery requires pinning v1 by hand, reloading, or something that reloads the hidden library page. Deleting in this tab does not trigger it, because the deletion leaves for the library, which then reloads.
- **Evidence:** :38-40 `const newer = (listed?.publishedVersion ?? 0) > (current.publishedVersion ?? 0); return listed && newer ? listed : current;`; :81-84 `Math.max(rules.find((rule) => rule.id === id)?.publishedVersion ?? 0, selectedRule?.publishedVersion ?? 0)`; :100 `current.id === id && current.version >= newestOnPage ? current : …`; :167-170 prepends `selectedRule`. Browser probe on :3191 (spec deleted): "before: version v2", "re-created X published version: 1"; "(a) after new search: … version v2 | execute enabled true", alert "Published rule version not found: rv-v-del-fe1-pg-…-x v2"; "(b) after re-select: … version v2 | execute enabled false", alert "…not found: … v2 / Retry loading", "curl has version 2: true"; "(c) after retry: version v2", same alert.
- **Suggested fix:** Scope every "newest known release" to the rule incarnation, using the shared `sameRule` helper from X41. (1) `selectionAfterCatalogPage` adopts the listed summary whenever it is another incarnation (`!sameRule(listed, current)`), and otherwise keeps the newer-only rule. (2) `listedVersion` counts the library row only when `sameRule(row, selectedRule)`. (3) `historyNewest` stores `{ id, createdAt, version }`, with the `createdAt` of the selected rule when its history page was read, and is used only for that incarnation (history rows carry no `createdAt`). For a deletion without re-creation, treat a 404 from the version read or from Execute (`ApiError.status === 404`; see X51) as "the rule is gone": clear `selectedRule` and `pinnedVersion` so the next catalog page selects again and the prepend at 167-170 stops offering it; let Retry reset `historyNewest` too. Refine F5's rule to "within one rule incarnation; a re-created rule replaces the selection".
- **Regression test:** frontend/tests/execution-catalog.spec.ts (mocked routes, like "fresh published catalog wins over a stale version in the parent library page"): the library and catalog first list X (`createdAt` t1, publishedVersion 2); then the catalog lists X with `createdAt` t2 and publishedVersion 1, and its history lists v1. After a new search and after re-selecting X, assert Version v1, an Execute request carrying version 1 and a cURL with `"version": 1`; a 404 from the version read clears the selection. Optionally export `selectionAfterCatalogPage` and unit-test it in frontend/tests/unit/execution.spec.ts.
- **Related:** X41, X01, X44 (same root cause); X51 (no production code reads `ApiError.status`); lesson F5; duplicate X115 (registry).

<a id="x42"></a>

### X42 · medium · bug · Leaving during a pending deletion aborts the DELETE client-side, so the deleted rule is never forgotten
- **Status:** CONFIRMED — record: `verdicts/v-del-fe2.md`
- **Area / module / feature:** Frontend / features/editor (`useRuleDocument.deleteRule`) and app (`App.forgetDeletedRule`, `useCodeStudioTarget.forget`, `useRuleLibrary.markChanged`) / rule deletion from Rule settings
- **Location:** `frontend/src/features/editor/useRuleDocument.ts:409` (+ `frontend/src/features/editor/useRuleDocument.ts:75`, `frontend/src/features/editor/useRuleDocument.ts:414`, `frontend/src/features/editor/editorCapabilities.ts:82`, `frontend/src/App.tsx:95`, `docs/maintaining.md:131`)
- **Problem:** The DELETE request carries the editor-session signal, and a pending deletion registers no navigation guard (`pendingWriteWarning` returns null for "delete": "A deletion does not ask"). Leaving the editor unmounts it, which aborts the session (75-79) and with it the fetch, while the server still deletes the rule. The catch returns early (410-412), so `onDeleted` (App's `forgetDeletedRule`) never runs. The comment at 414-415 ("The rule is gone even when the user has left the editor meanwhile") and docs/maintaining.md:131 ("Once the server has deleted it, the editor reports `onDeleted` … `App` forgets the rule's saved copy, library page and Code studio target") promise the opposite; the branch they describe cannot run in practice.
- **Trigger → consequence:** Open a clean rule from the library → Rule settings → Delete rule… → Delete rule → press browser Back (or Escape and a sidebar link) before the 204 arrives. No prompt appears and the server deletes the rule. Afterwards there is no "Rule X deleted" notice; `library.markChanged` never runs, so the library keeps listing the deleted rule; `codeStudio.forget` never runs, so sidebar Code studio opens `#/studio/<deleted>` → "Could not load rule: Rule not found"; and `savedRule` is not cleared. The trigger needs a leave within the DELETE round trip.
- **Evidence:** useRuleDocument.ts:408-416 `try { await ruleApi.delete(rule.id, { signal }); } catch (failure) { if (!signal.aborted) refusal = errorMessage(failure); return; } // The rule is gone even when the user has left the editor meanwhile; ... onDeleted?.(rule.id);`; lines 75-79 abort the session on unmount; editorCapabilities.ts:82-90 "A deletion does not ask" and `return null` for "delete". Browser probe on :3191 (route.fetch performed the real DELETE and its response was held, then `history.back()`): `X42 prompts []`, `server GET after 404`, `deleted notice count 0`, `library still lists deleted rule 1`, `url after Code studio /studio/rv-v-del-fe2-a-…`, `main text … Could not load rule: Rule not found: rv-v-del-fe2-a-… Retry rule`.
- **Suggested fix:** Let the deletion outlive the editor session, as documented. Send `ruleApi.delete(rule.id)` without the session signal (once sent, a deletion cannot be recalled), and after it resolves always call `onDeleted(rule.id)` and the App-level `notify("Rule X deleted")`; keep only `onDirty(false)` and `navigate("/library")` conditional on `!signal.aborted`. A refusal or failure that arrives after the session closed has no dialog left, so report it through `notify` (e.g. "Rule X was not deleted: <message>"; see X91). Make `forgetDeletedRule` use a functional update (`setSavedRule((saved) => (saved?.id === id ? null : saved))`), because it can now run after App has re-rendered with another saved copy. The alternative, adding "delete" to `pendingWriteWarning` so that leaving asks as it does for save and publish, contradicts the comment and maintaining.md:131; choose it only if leaving should be able to abandon a deletion.
- **Regression test:** frontend/tests/rule-delete.spec.ts: route the DELETE with `route.fetch()` and hold the fulfilment, press Back, then release; assert no prompt, the snackbar "Rule X deleted", no card for X once the library is shown, sidebar Code studio not opening `#/studio/X`, and `GET /api/rules/X` → 404. Add the late-failure variant (a held 409 released after leaving shows the refusal in the snackbar), as frontend/AGENTS.md asks for late-response cases.
- **Related:** X91 (same "outcome delivered only to an unmounted UI" gap), X51 (the stale Code studio target lands on the retryable 404 page), X45; lessons F3 (save and publish register a guard because "an aborted request may still have reached the server"), F23, B26; duplicates X49, X88, X94, X114, X120 (registry).

<a id="x81"></a>

### X81 · medium · bug · The delete confirmation uses a stale `publishedVersion`, and DELETE has no precondition, so a rule published elsewhere is deleted behind the draft-only confirmation
- **Status:** CONFIRMED — record: `verdicts/v-del-fe2.md`
- **Area / module / feature:** Frontend / features/editor (`RuleSettings` `DeleteRule`), with backend `rule.RuleService` and `api.RuleController` / rule deletion (confirmation and write precondition)
- **Location:** `frontend/src/features/editor/RuleSettings.tsx:128` (+ `frontend/src/features/editor/RuleSettings.tsx:158`, `frontend/src/features/editor/RuleSettings.tsx:179`, `frontend/src/api/rules.ts:58`, `backend/src/main/java/dev/arc/rule/RuleService.java:120`, `backend/src/main/java/dev/arc/rule/RuleService.java:95`, `backend/src/main/java/dev/arc/rule/RuleService.java:108`, `backend/src/main/java/dev/arc/api/RuleController.java:63`)
- **Problem:** The delete dialog takes "published" from the editor's document copy of `rule.publishedVersion`, which nothing refreshes except this editor's own save and publish acknowledgements. That value decides the versions warning and the "Type X to confirm" field (158-170) and whether a typed ID is required (179). `DELETE /api/rules/{id}` carries no revision or other precondition: `RuleService.delete` locks, checks callers and deletes (120-131) without `revision(...)`, unlike update (95-96) and publish (108-109). The client therefore cannot make its confirmation match what the server will delete.
- **Trigger → consequence:** Editor A opens unpublished rule X. Another tab, user or API client publishes X v1, and execute returns 200. In A: Rule settings → Delete rule…. The settings say "Publish this rule to make it available through the execution API", the alert says "Deleting removes this draft. This cannot be undone.", no "Type X to confirm" field appears, and Delete is enabled. Clicking it deletes the draft and v1, so API callers now get 404. Save and publish would have hit a 409 on the stale revision (publish bumps it); delete has no equivalent.
- **Evidence:** RuleSettings.tsx:128 `const published = rule.publishedVersion;`; api/rules.ts ``delete: (id, options) => http.delete<void>(`/rules/${pathId(id)}`, options)`` sends no revision; RuleService.java:120-131 is `store.lock(id); callersOf...; store.delete(id);` with no `revision(...)`. Browser probe: `X81 publish elsewhere 200`, `execute before 200 … "result":9.0`, `settings copy … Publish this rule to make it available through the execution API.`, `alert Deleting removes this draft. This cannot be undone.`, `type-id fields 0`, `confirm enabled true`, `GET versions/1 after 404`, `execute after 404`.
- **Suggested fix:** Give DELETE the optimistic precondition that save and publish already have. Backend: `RuleController.delete` binds `@RequestParam(required = false) Integer revision`; `RuleService.delete(id, revision)` calls `revision(rule, revision)` right after `store.lock(id)`, inside the same transaction, whenever it is supplied, and answers 409 (parameterize the message, which now says "Reload it before saving or publishing"). Frontend: `ruleApi.delete(id, revision, options)` sends the document's `rule.revision`; the dialog shows the 409 and tells the user to reload, so the next attempt shows the published warning and the type-the-ID step. Because publish advances the revision, a publication made elsewhere always fails the check. Optionally also re-read the rule when "Delete rule…" opens so the warning is current before the user confirms. Compatibility: clients that omit `revision` keep today's behavior; document the parameter under docs/api.md "Delete a rule" and add a "Behavior changes" entry (the status table already lists 409 for a stale revision). With X41 option (a), the same check also rejects a stale incarnation.
- **Regression test:** Backend `RuleServiceTest`: delete with a stale revision throws 409 and keeps the rule and its versions; delete with the current revision or with none succeeds. `RuleDeletionWebTest`: `DELETE /api/rules/{id}?revision=<stale>` → 409 with the standard error body, `?revision=<current>` → 204. scripts/smoke.py: read X, publish it, then DELETE with the revision read before → 409, and version 1 still executes. frontend/tests/rule-delete.spec.ts: open unpublished X, publish it through the API, confirm the draft-only deletion → the dialog shows the conflict and `GET /api/rules/X/versions/1` is still 200.
- **Related:** X41 (revisions are not incarnation-safe; its option (a) strengthens this check), X91, X42; lesson B26; maintainability lesson "Keep lock → revision check → validation → publication inside the write transaction"; AGENTS.md "Draft save and publish retain revision checks and atomic transactions".

<a id="x91"></a>

### X91 · low · bug · Rule settings can be closed while a deletion is pending, and a refusal is then shown nowhere
- **Status:** CONFIRMED — record: `verdicts/v-del-fe2.md`
- **Area / module / feature:** Frontend / features/editor (`RuleSettings`, `useRuleDocument.deleteRule`) / rule deletion from Rule settings
- **Location:** `frontend/src/features/editor/RuleSettings.tsx:36` (+ `frontend/src/features/editor/RuleSettings.tsx:87`, `frontend/src/features/editor/RuleSettings.tsx:151`, `frontend/src/features/editor/RuleSettings.tsx:173`, `frontend/src/features/editor/useRuleDocument.ts:410`, `frontend/src/features/editor/Editor.tsx:392`, `frontend/src/features/editor/NodeExpressionDialog.tsx:61`)
- **Problem:** The dialog's `onClose` (Escape, backdrop) and its footer button stay enabled while DeleteRule's request runs; only "Keep rule" is disabled (173). `deleteRule` catches every failure itself and returns it only to the dialog (`refusal`), so it never reaches `runTask`'s `fail()` or the editor's error banner. Once the dialog has closed, a server refusal (409 callers, 500, network error) is written into the unmounted DeleteRule (151-153) and shown nowhere. While the deletion is pending, `readOnly={!can.edit}` (Editor.tsx:392) even relabels Cancel to "Close". NodeExpressionDialog blocks closing while busy (line 61).
- **Trigger → consequence:** Take a published rule that another rule calls → Delete rule… → type the ID → Delete rule → press Escape before the 409 arrives. The dialog closes. The refusal "Other rules call this rule: …" never appears: there is no editor error banner, and reopening settings shows nothing. The rule still exists and the user is not told why nothing happened, although docs/studio.md:287 promises "A rule that other rules call stays, and the dialog names the callers."
- **Evidence:** RuleSettings.tsx:36 `onClose={onClose}` and :87 `<Button onClick={onClose}>{readOnly ? "Close" : "Cancel"}</Button>` are always enabled; :173 `<Button onClick={cancel} disabled={deleting}>` (Keep rule); :151-153 `const reason = await onDelete(); setDeleting(false); if (reason) setRefusal(reason);`; useRuleDocument.ts:410-412 `catch (failure) { if (!signal.aborted) refusal = errorMessage(failure); return; }`. Browser probe (real DELETE → 409, response held): `dialog button while pending: Deleting… 1, Close 1`, `closed by Escape`, then after release `refusal visible anywhere 0`, `alerts []`, `reopened refusal 0`, `GET callee 200`.
- **Suggested fix:** Treat a pending deletion like the other pending dialog commands: pass `deleting={busy === "delete"}` from Editor (or lift DeleteRule's `deleting` state into RuleSettings), set `onClose={deleting ? undefined : onClose}` and disable the footer Cancel/Close while it runs, as NodeExpressionDialog.tsx:61 and :114 do. The refusal then always lands in the mounted dialog. As a fallback for a session that closes anyway (X42), `deleteRule` reports a refusal that no mounted dialog can show through `notify`. This also removes the X95 variant (on success the editor navigated to the library although the user had pressed Close).
- **Regression test:** frontend/tests/rule-delete.spec.ts, "a published rule asks for its ID, and a rule that another rule calls is kept": hold the DELETE response with a route and assert that Escape, a backdrop click and the footer button leave the dialog open while "Deleting…" shows; release it and assert the refusal text is visible in the dialog. With the X42 fallback, leaving during a held 409 shows the refusal in the snackbar.
- **Related:** X42 (same outcome lost when its UI unmounts), X45 (the refusal names only five callers); maintainability lesson on the command gate ("callers move the selection or close a dialog only after an accepted edit"); lesson B26; duplicates X95, X113 (registry).

<a id="x45"></a>

### X45 · low · bug · ApiError drops the error body's `issues`, so a refused deletion never shows callers beyond the first five
- **Status:** CONFIRMED — record: `verdicts/v-del-fe2.md`
- **Area / module / feature:** Frontend / api (`http.ts`, `errors.ts`) and features/editor (`RuleSettings`) / rule deletion refused because other rules call the rule
- **Location:** `frontend/src/api/http.ts:49` (+ `frontend/src/api/errors.ts:11`, `frontend/src/features/editor/useRuleDocument.ts:411`, `backend/src/main/java/dev/arc/rule/RuleService.java:29`, `docs/api.md:99`)
- **Problem:** `requestFailure` builds ApiError from `message`, `locations` and the status only, and ApiError(message, locations, status) has no `issues` field; no code in frontend/src reads an error body's `issues`. The server deliberately names at most five callers in the message and lists all of them in `issues` (RuleService.java:29, "How many callers a refused deletion names in its message; `issues` lists them all"; docs/api.md:99). Library search covers only id, name and description (JdbcRuleRepository.java:17-19), so the remaining callers cannot be found in the UI.
- **Trigger → consequence:** A published rule is called by 7 drafts. DELETE returns 409 with `issues` listing all 7 and a message naming 5 "and 2 more". The Rule settings dialog shows only the message, and callers 6 and 7 appear nowhere; the user can only remove the named callers and retry to find the others.
- **Evidence:** http.ts:49-55 `return new ApiError(body.message ? String(body.message) : …, Array.isArray(body.locations) ? body.locations : [], status);`; errors.ts:11-20. Probe: curl DELETE → `status 409`, `"issues":[caller1 … caller7]` (7 entries), and a message ending "caller5 (draft) and 2 more. Remove those calls before deleting it."; in the browser the dialog alert shows the same 5-name message, and `caller6/7 shown anywhere 0 0`.
- **Suggested fix:** Keep `issues` at the HTTP boundary: add `readonly issues: string[]` to ApiError (default `[]`) and fill it in `requestFailure` from `body.issues` when it is an array of strings. Have `deleteRule` return a structured refusal (`{ message, callers }`, with `callers = failure.issues` for an `ApiError` whose status is 409) and let DeleteRule list every caller under its alert. Show `issues` only where their meaning is known: for most errors they merely repeat the message (docs/api.md, Errors).
- **Regression test:** frontend/tests/unit/http.spec.ts, "API errors retain graph locations and status; non-JSON errors still explain failure": assert that `issues` is kept, and is `[]` for a non-JSON or issue-less body. frontend/tests/rule-delete.spec.ts: a callee called by 7 caller drafts (API fixtures) → the dialog shows all 7 caller names.
- **Related:** X91, X42 (deletion refusal reporting); X51, X02 (`ApiError.status`, the other error field the UI never reads); lesson B26 (the 409 lists every caller); duplicate X117 (registry).

<a id="x51"></a>

### X51 · low · bug · A missing or deleted rule is shown as a retryable load failure; the "Rule not found / Back to library" branch is dead
- **Status:** CONFIRMED — record: `verdicts/v-del-fe2.md`
- **Area / module / feature:** Frontend / app (`App.tsx` `ruleContent`) and hooks (`useAsyncResource`) / opening a rule route for a deleted or unknown rule
- **Location:** `frontend/src/App.tsx:125` (+ `frontend/src/App.tsx:134`, `frontend/src/hooks/useAsyncResource.ts:53`, `frontend/src/api/errors.ts:15`, `frontend/src/app/routing.ts:28`, `frontend/tests/style-equivalence.spec.ts:360`)
- **Problem:** useAsyncResource reduces every failure to a string (`errorMessage(error)`), and no production code reads `ApiError.status`. App checks `detail.error` before the not-found branch, so a 404 renders "Could not load rule: …" with a "Retry rule" button that can never succeed. The "Rule not found / Back to library" branch (134-139) renders only if GET answers 2xx with a null or empty body, which the backend never does, and the route regex `([^/]+)` never yields an empty ruleId. This dates back to the baseline (the same error-before-not-found order existed at 49bb365), but deletion now makes 404 routes common: Back after deleting, stale cards and Code studio targets (X42).
- **Trigger → consequence:** Delete a rule, which navigates to #/library, then press Back, or open any `#/rules/<unknown>`. GET returns 404, `detail.error` is set, and the page shows the error alert and "Retry rule"; Retry refetches and shows the same error. No "Back to library" action appears.
- **Evidence:** App.tsx:124-139 checks `if (detail.loading) … if (detail.error) return (… Could not load rule: {detail.error} … Retry rule …)` before `<h2>Rule not found</h2> <Button onClick={() => navigate("/library")}>Back to library</Button>`; useAsyncResource.ts:53 `error: errorMessage(error),`; routing.ts:28 `/^\/(rules|studio)\/([^/]+)$/`. style-equivalence.spec.ts:360 `getByText("Rule not found")` passes only because it matches a substring of the alert. Browser probe `#/rules/rv-v-del-fe2-missing`: `main text … Could not load rule: Rule not found: rv-v-del-fe2-missing Retry rule`, `h2 Rule not found 0`, `Back to library 0`, `Retry rule 1`, and the same text after Retry.
- **Suggested fix:** Let the resource hook keep the failure's status: add `status: number | null` to `Resource<T>`, set from `error instanceof ApiError ? error.status : null` in useAsyncResource's catch. In `App.ruleContent`, check `detail.status === 404` before the generic error branch and render the existing "Rule not found" heading with "Back to library" and no Retry; keep "Could not load rule … Retry rule" for other failures; delete the unreachable null-body fallback. Change style-equivalence.spec.ts:360 to wait for `getByRole("heading", { name: "Rule not found" })` and re-record that snapshot on purpose, because the markup changes. Add a docs/studio.md "Behavior changes" line: an unknown or deleted rule shows "Rule not found" with "Back to library" instead of a retry. X02 can reuse the same status to detect a deleted rule.
- **Regression test:** frontend/tests/rule-delete.spec.ts: after a deletion, go Back → the heading "Rule not found" is shown, "Retry rule" is not, and "Back to library" navigates to #/library. frontend/tests/resources.spec.ts: a mocked 500 on the rule detail still offers "Retry rule", while a 404 shows the not-found state.
- **Related:** X42 (a stale target lands here), X02, X45 (ApiError fields the UI never reads); lesson F5 (explicit loading/ready/failed resource states; not found is its own terminal state); duplicates X116, X123, X138 (registry).
