### X182 · low · cleanup · Paged version select written three times and already drifting
- **Status:** CONFIRMED — record: `verdicts/v-clean-fe2.md`
- **Area / module / feature:** Frontend / features/sources (SourceBindingEditor.tsx, SourcesPage.tsx), features/execution (ApiPage.tsx), components / version pickers: input source version, Sources "Inspect version", playground "Version"
- **Location:** `frontend/src/features/sources/SourceBindingEditor.tsx:134` (+ `frontend/src/features/sources/SourcesPage.tsx:183`, `frontend/src/features/execution/ApiPage.tsx:134`, `frontend/src/features/editor/inspector/ReferenceFields.tsx:114`)
- **Problem:** The same paged version control is written out three times: a MUI select over one history page, plus an extra option for a selected version that is not on the loaded page, followed by `CatalogPagination` fed from the same paged resource. The three copies already differ in option labels, null guards and disabled/loading rules. Reference nodes pin versions with a different idiom, a searchable `PagedAutocomplete`.
- **Trigger → consequence:** Nothing breaks functionally today. Any change to the control has to be made three times: a pending indicator while a chosen version loads, the latest marker, or a move to searchable paging like the Reference picker. One drift is already visible on the Sources page: the " · latest"/" · immutable" suffix is added only to options on the loaded page, not to the off-page option, so the closed select shows "vN · latest" on page 1 but only "vN" after the user pages to an older page while viewing the latest version.
- **Evidence:** SourceBindingEditor.tsx:134-157: `label="Source version" value={source.version} disabled={readOnly || versionsResource.loading}`, `{!versions.some((item) => item.version === source.version) && (<MenuItem value={source.version}>v{source.version}</MenuItem>)}`, `<CatalogPagination label="Source versions" … loading={versionsResource.loading} …/>`. SourcesPage.tsx:183-219: `label="Inspect version" value={document.viewedVersion} disabled={saving || editor.versionsLoading}`, the same off-page `<MenuItem value={document.viewedVersion}>v{document.viewedVersion}</MenuItem>`, `{version.version === selected.version ? " · latest" : " · immutable"}`, `<CatalogPagination label="Source history" …/>`. ApiPage.tsx:134-159: `label="Version" value={version ?? ""} disabled={running || request.history.loading || !id}`, `{version !== null && !versions.some((candidate) => candidate.version === version) && <MenuItem value={version}>v{version}</MenuItem>}`, `<CatalogPagination label="Published versions" … loading={request.history.loading || !id} …/>`. The other idiom: ReferenceFields.tsx:114-141 `<PagedAutocomplete<VersionSummary> label="Pinned version" …>`. No doc names this repetition as intended.
- **Suggested fix:** Add `frontend/src/components/PagedVersionSelect.tsx` (feature-independent, MUI only) with props `label`, `value: number | null`, `versions: readonly { version: number }[]`, `disabled`, `optionLabel?: (version: number) => string` (default `` `v${version}` ``) and `onChange(version: number)`. It renders the select, maps `null` to `""`, and renders the off-page selected option through the same `optionLabel`, which fixes the suffix drift. Keep `CatalogPagination` as a sibling at each call site, because SourcesPage places the select inside `.source-form-grid` and the pagination below the grid (SourcesPage.tsx:211-220); a small `paginationProps(resource)` helper next to `usePagedResource` can supply `offset`, `limit`, `total`, `loading` and `onPage` to the three callers. Migrate SourceBindingEditor.tsx:134-149, SourcesPage.tsx:184-208 (passing the latest/immutable `optionLabel`) and ApiPage.tsx:134-150. Decision needed: whether source pins and the playground should later adopt the Reference idiom (searchable `PagedAutocomplete`); recommend the shared select now, which preserves behavior, and a separate decision about searchable pickers. The off-page suffix is a small visible fix; no docs entry needed.
- **Regression test:** `frontend/tests/source-editor.spec.ts`: after paging "Source history pages" to an older page while viewing the latest version, the closed "Inspect version" select still reads "vN · latest". Keep `frontend/tests/execution-catalog.spec.ts` ("Published versions pages", history paging), `frontend/tests/frontend-refactor.spec.ts` (pinned source parameter mapping) and `frontend/tests/source-provider.spec.ts` green.
- **Related:** X06 and X172 (same ApiPage), X50 (usePagedResource restores an old offset for a returning key), X221 (SourceBindingEditor re-reads versions on every selection); lesson F5 (an explicit pin stays separate from the loaded page; an off-page pinned version stays selected).

### X183 · low · cleanup · Typed-text buffer (F27) and refuse-and-explain guard (F28/F34) each hand-written twice; IdentifierField keeps a stale refusal
- **Status:** CONFIRMED — record: `verdicts/v-clean-fe3.md`
- **Area / module / feature:** Frontend / components (JsonField.tsx, ResourceIdField.tsx), features/editor/inspector (NumericDefaultField.tsx, IdentifierField.tsx) / ARRAY/OBJECT and NUMBER parameter defaults, parameter and result-variable names, Rule and Source ID fields
- **Location:** `frontend/src/components/JsonField.tsx:27` (+ `frontend/src/features/editor/inspector/NumericDefaultField.tsx:22`, `frontend/src/components/JsonField.tsx:53`, `frontend/src/features/editor/inspector/NumericDefaultField.tsx:45`, `frontend/src/features/editor/inspector/IdentifierField.tsx:36`, `frontend/src/features/editor/inspector/IdentifierField.tsx:56`, `frontend/src/components/ResourceIdField.tsx:25`, `frontend/src/components/ResourceIdField.tsx:46`)
- **Problem:** Two field policies are each written out twice. JsonField and NumericDefaultField both hand-write the F27 parsed-text buffer: a validity ref reset on unmount, text and error state, an `acceptedValue` check that ignores the field's own echo, and the same onChange sequence. IdentifierField and ResourceIdField both hand-write the refuse-and-explain paste guard. The refusal pair has already diverged: IdentifierField's sticky `rejectedEdit` keeps a now-valid name marked invalid after the value changes from outside, while ResourceIdField ties a refusal to the value it left in place.
- **Trigger → consequence:** (1) Buffer pair: the policy has drifted once already. At aca6775 NumericDefaultField already had the acceptedValue check, while JsonField at 49bb365 still rewrote its text on every `encoded` change, which caused the F27 caret bug. Any future fix has to be made in both files, and both are rendered only by InputDefaultValue.tsx. (2) Refusal pair, reproduced: select the Formula node and paste "bad name" into the sidebar Result variable; it is refused and shows the error. Then right-click the card, choose Edit, set Result variable to "grand_total" and click Apply to graph. The sidebar field now shows "grand_total" with aria-invalid=true and only the red guidance text, until the next keystroke in that field. The error is cosmetic: Save is not blocked, since IdentifierField reports no validity. It contradicts F28 in review-lessons.md:42: "the field shows the guidance until the name is valid."
- **Evidence:** Same scaffold in JsonField.tsx:27-42 and NumericDefaultField.tsx:22-35: `const acceptedValue = useRef(value);` … `if (sameJson(value, acceptedValue.current)) return;` vs `if (sameNumericDefault(value, acceptedValue.current)) return;`; onChange handlers JsonField.tsx:53-68 and NumericDefaultField.tsx:45-55. History: `git show 49bb365:frontend/src/components/JsonField.tsx` has `useEffect(() => { setText(encoded ? …) … }, [encoded]);`, while aca6775's NumericDefaultField.tsx:23 has `if (Object.is(value, acceptedValue.current)) return;`. The paste guard is identical at IdentifierField.tsx:56 and ResourceIdField.tsx:46: `if (/[\s$@]/u.test(event.clipboardData.getData("text"))) {`. IdentifierField.tsx:36-37 `const [rejectedEdit, setRejectedEdit] = useState(false); const error = fieldError(value, optional, rejectedEdit);`, reset only by onChange (:51); ResourceIdField.tsx:25-27 `// A refusal belongs to the value it left in place; any later value clears it.` and `const refused = refusedAt === value;`. The sidebar Inspector stays mounted while NodeEditDialog is open (Editor.tsx:332/336 `readOnly={!can.edit || !!activeDialog}`), and ResultFields is keyed `result:${node.id}` (Inspector.tsx:132). Browser probe on the disposable stack (port 3191, spec zz-v-clean-fe3-sticky, since deleted with its rule): before the paste the helper read "Use this variable in later nodes. Use letters, …"; after the paste and the dialog rename it logged `{"value":"grand_total","invalid":"true","helperAfter":"Use letters, digits, or _; start with a letter or _. No spaces, $ or @. Maximum 64 characters.","helperIsError":1}`; screenshot $SP/pw-v-clean-fe3/zz-v-clean-fe3-sticky-side-7b333-ter-a-valid-external-rename/sticky.png shows a red field around "grand_total".
- **Suggested fix:** Two shared hooks in `frontend/src/hooks/` (components/ may import hooks/). (1) `useParsedTextBuffer<T>({ value, format, parse, same, onChange, onValidity })`, where `parse(raw)` returns `{ valid: true; value: T } | { valid: false; error: string }`; the hook owns the text and error state, the `acceptedValue` ref, the echo-ignoring `[value]` effect, the unmount validity reset and a `change(raw)` handler. JsonField passes `format = jsonText`, `same = sameJson` and a parse that maps a `parseJson` failure to "Enter valid JSON before saving" (empty text is `null`); NumericDefaultField passes `defaultText`, `sameNumericDefault` and `parseNumericDefault`. Keep `format`, `same` and `parse` module-level so the effect dependencies stay stable. (2) `useRefusedEdit(value)` returning `{ refused, refuse(), clear(), onPaste }` with ResourceIdField's semantics (`refusedAt === value`) and one named paste rule for `/[\s$@]/u`. IdentifierField replaces `rejectedEdit` with it, which fixes the stale error because the refusal ends when the value changes from outside; ResourceIdField uses it unchanged. Optionally, move JsonField.tsx:72's inline `fontFamily: "JetBrains Mono, monospace", fontSize: 12` into a class that reads `--font-mono` (the finder noted it restates the token). Update the sentence on `NumericDefaultField` and `JsonField` in docs/maintaining.md:149 to name the hook. The refusal fix is a small visible change; no compatibility entry needed.
- **Regression test:** `frontend/tests/node-names.spec.ts`: paste "bad name" into the sidebar Result variable (refused, guidance shown), rename it to "grand_total" through the node edit dialog and Apply, then assert the sidebar field has `aria-invalid="false"` and the non-error helper text. Keep `frontend/tests/json-defaults.spec.ts` (F27: typing, external changes, clearing), `frontend/tests/create-rule.spec.ts` and `frontend/tests/source-editor.spec.ts` (ResourceIdField) green.
- **Related:** Lessons F27, F28 and F34; X57 (JsonField marks any parseable JSON valid); history 49bb365 and aca6775, pattern copied in 133ca20.

### X187 · low · cleanup · useAsyncResource/usePagedResource take delay/enabled positionally: placeholder arguments and 9 non-null assertions tied to a guard two arguments away
- **Status:** CONFIRMED — record: `verdicts/v-clean-fe3.md`
- **Area / module / feature:** Frontend / hooks (useAsyncResource.ts, usePagedResource.ts) and their callers / every keyed read: rule detail, library and catalogs, version histories, Reference and source bindings, node code
- **Location:** `frontend/src/hooks/useAsyncResource.ts:16` (+ `frontend/src/hooks/usePagedResource.ts:18`, `frontend/src/App.tsx:71`, `frontend/src/features/editor/inspector/ReferenceFields.tsx:31`, `frontend/src/features/editor/inspector/ReferenceFields.tsx:46`, `frontend/src/features/sources/SourceBindingEditor.tsx:42`, `frontend/src/features/sources/SourceBindingEditor.tsx:47`, `frontend/src/features/sources/useSourceEditor.ts:79`, `frontend/src/features/editor/useNodeExpressionDraft.ts:45`, `frontend/src/features/execution/usePublishedExecution.ts:111`)
- **Problem:** `useAsyncResource` and `usePagedResource` take `delay`/`enabled` as trailing positional parameters. Callers therefore pass a placeholder `0` in 8 places and a positional `true` in 3, and 9 non-null assertions in loaders depend on an `enabled` expression written two arguments later. One loader also carries a dead null branch that exists only for narrowing.
- **Trigger → consequence:** No guard is misaligned today. All 9 assertions are covered by their `enabled` expression, and the app uses no startTransition/useTransition/useDeferredValue, so the loader ref cannot run with an uncommitted closure. The cost is compiler bypass and readability: the `!` assertions are hidden casts whose soundness rests on a separate argument, so changing one side without the other would send, for example, `/api/rules/null` with no type error. frontend/AGENTS.md:20: "Use readable local names instead of cast chains or `any` to satisfy the compiler."
- **Evidence:** useAsyncResource.ts:16-23 `(key, load, initial, delay = 0, enabled = true, { keepPrevious = false } = {})`. Placeholder `0`: App.tsx:73, RuleCard.tsx:30, useFunctionCatalog.ts:34, SourceBindingEditor.tsx:49, usePublishedExecution.ts:119, ReferenceFields.tsx:33 and :48, usePagedResource.ts:36 (8). Positional `true`: useRuleLibrary.ts:25, usePublishedExecution.ts:68, useSourceEditor.ts:51 (3). Assertions: App.tsx:71 `ruleApi.get(ruleId!, { signal })` with :74 `!!ruleId`; ReferenceFields.tsx:31 `node.ruleId!` and :46 `node.ruleId!, node.version!`; SourceBindingEditor.tsx:42 `source!.id` and :47 `source!.id, source!.version`; useSourceEditor.ts:79 `selected!.id`; useNodeExpressionDraft.ts:45 `source!` (9 total). Dead branch: usePublishedExecution.ts:111-114 `version === null ? Promise.resolve(null) : …`, although :120 has `!!id && version !== null`.
- **Suggested fix:** Change the hook contract so the loader carries its own guard: `useAsyncResource<T>(key, load: ((signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<T>) | null, initial, { delay = 0, keepPrevious = false } = {})`, with `enabled = load !== null` feeding the same effect dependencies (`[key, delay, enabled, keepData]`), so reads fire at exactly the same moments and a changed loader closure still does not refetch (frontend/AGENTS.md: keys drive reads). Likewise `usePagedResource<T>(key, load: ((offset, limit, signal) => Promise<Page<T>>) | null, { refresh, keepPrevious } = {})`, passing `load ? (signal) => load(offset, limit, signal) : null` down. Call sites narrow through const locals, which keep their narrowing inside closures (property paths such as `node.ruleId` do not): App.tsx `ruleId ? (signal) => ruleApi.get(ruleId, { signal }) : null`; ReferenceFields `const { ruleId, version } = node;` then `ruleId && version ? (signal) => ruleApi.version(ruleId, version, { signal }) : null`; the same for SourceBindingEditor, useSourceEditor and useNodeExpressionDraft. Delete the 8 placeholders, the 3 positional `true`s, the 9 assertions and the dead branch at usePublishedExecution.ts:111-114. Update the `hooks/` row in docs/maintaining.md:109. No behavior change.
- **Regression test:** `npm --prefix frontend run build` (tsc) with no `!` left in these loaders, plus the existing workflows that exercise keyed reads and late responses: `frontend/tests/resources.spec.ts` (stale diagnostics, version failure and retry), `frontend/tests/execution-catalog.spec.ts` (single first-page history read), `frontend/tests/catalog-pagination.spec.ts`, `frontend/tests/reference-picker.spec.ts` and `frontend/tests/source-provider.spec.ts`.
- **Related:** X219 (useAsyncResource commits a second render per key change), X50 (usePagedResource restores an old offset for a returning key), X221 (the same reads in SourceBindingEditor and ReferenceFields), X201 (ReferenceFields' `!knownRule` guard); frontend/AGENTS.md:20.

### X189 · low · cleanup · Embedded source manager mirrors the editor's dirty/busy state through effect callbacks; discard prompt worded two ways
- **Status:** CONFIRMED — record: `verdicts/v-clean-fe3.md`
- **Area / module / feature:** Frontend / features/sources (SourceManagerDialog.tsx, SourcesPage.tsx, useSourceEditor.ts) / "Manage data sources" dialog and the Data sources page
- **Location:** `frontend/src/features/sources/SourceManagerDialog.tsx:17` (+ `frontend/src/features/sources/useSourceEditor.ts:99`, `frontend/src/features/sources/useSourceEditor.ts:282`, `frontend/src/features/sources/SourcesPage.tsx:35`, `frontend/src/features/sources/SourceManagerDialog.tsx:42`, `frontend/src/features/sources/useSourceEditor.ts:31`, `frontend/src/features/sources/useSourceEditor.ts:104`, `frontend/src/features/sources/SourceManagerDialog.tsx:23`)
- **Problem:** The embedded source manager copies the source editor's `dirty` and `pending` into its own `useState` through two effect-fired callbacks: `onDirty` in useSourceEditor and `onBusy` in SourcesPage. `pending` exists only to feed that copy. This is duplicate state synchronized by effects, which frontend/AGENTS.md:11 rules out ("derive values instead of synchronizing duplicate state with effects"). The discard prompt is worded two ways across three places.
- **Trigger → consequence:** The dialog's Close guard reads a copy set one commit later. That lag has no observable effect for clicks and keys, because React flushes discrete-event effects synchronously, so there is no behavior bug. The cost is extra plumbing: `onDirty`, `onBusy` and `pending` exist only for this dialog, and App's sources page passes neither callback. The wording has drifted: the navigation guard asks "Discard unsaved data source changes?", while selecting another source and closing the dialog ask "Discard unsaved source changes?". useRuleDocument.ts:110-112 uses the same effect-callback pattern for App's onDirty, so fixing this is a pattern decision.
- **Evidence:** useSourceEditor.ts:99-101 `useEffect(() => { onDirty?.(dirty); }, [dirty, onDirty]);` and :282 `pending: savingIds.length > 0 || document?.testing != null`, whose only reader is SourcesPage.tsx:35-37 `useEffect(() => { onBusy?.(editor.pending); }, [editor.pending, onBusy]);`. SourceManagerDialog.tsx:17-18 `const [dirty, setDirty] = useState(false); const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);` and :42 `<SourcesPage onDirty={setDirty} onBusy={setBusy} notify={setNotice} />`. App.tsx:147 `<SourcesPage notify={setNotice} />`. Prompts: useSourceEditor.ts:31 "Discard unsaved data source changes?" vs useSourceEditor.ts:104 and SourceManagerDialog.tsx:23 "Discard unsaved source changes?".
- **Suggested fix:** Lift the controller to the host, as `usePreviewExecution` and `TestPanel` already split state and presentation. Split SourcesPage into the route page (`const editor = useSourceEditor({ notify }); return <SourceWorkspace editor={editor} />`) and a presentational `SourceWorkspace({ editor })` holding today's page body. SourceManagerDialog calls `useSourceEditor({ notify: setNotice })` itself, renders `<SourceWorkspace editor={editor} />`, and its Close reads `editor.pending` and `editor.dirty` directly. Delete `onDirty` (useSourceEditor.ts:35-41, :99-101), `onBusy` (SourcesPage.tsx:23-37) and the dialog's two `useState`s. The editor's navigation guards stay registered by `useSourceEditor` wherever it is called. Export one `unsavedSourceWarning` ("Discard unsaved data source changes?", the text docs/studio.md already quotes) from useSourceEditor.ts and use it at :104 and in the dialog's Close. Decide separately whether App's `onDirty` (useRuleDocument.ts:110-112, feeding `useWorkspaceNavigation.setDirty`) moves to the same pattern; it is lower priority. The unified wording matches docs/studio.md already, so no new docs entry is needed.
- **Regression test:** Keep `frontend/tests/workspace-navigation.spec.ts` ("the embedded source manager guards Back, view switches and unloading") and `frontend/tests/source-provider.spec.ts` ("nested source management keeps its staged parent and blocks closing during any pending save") green; add assertions that "Close data sources" with unsaved edits and selecting another source both prompt exactly "Discard unsaved data source changes?".
- **Related:** Lesson F23 (the source editor registers its own navigation guards; the dialog must not mirror them); the same effect-callback pattern at useRuleDocument.ts:110-112.

### X190 · low · cleanup · `presentation` and `hideInput` flags make props meaningless (NodeEditDialog passes `errors={[]}`)
- **Status:** CONFIRMED — record: `verdicts/v-clean-fe3.md`
- **Area / module / feature:** Frontend / features/editor/inspector (Inspector.tsx, ConditionFields.tsx, TransformFields.tsx), features/editor/NodeEditDialog.tsx, features/expressions/ExpressionField.tsx / inspector sidebar vs node edit dialog; Condition builder and Transform "open editor" buttons
- **Location:** `frontend/src/features/editor/inspector/Inspector.tsx:44` (+ `frontend/src/features/editor/inspector/Inspector.tsx:52`, `frontend/src/features/editor/inspector/Inspector.tsx:90`, `frontend/src/features/editor/NodeEditDialog.tsx:113`, `frontend/src/features/editor/NodeEditDialog.tsx:120`, `frontend/src/features/expressions/ExpressionField.tsx:34`, `frontend/src/features/editor/inspector/ConditionFields.tsx:189`, `frontend/src/features/editor/inspector/TransformFields.tsx:96`)
- **Problem:** Two flags make some props meaningless. Inspector's `presentation: "sidebar" | "dialog"` hides the heading and footer, so the required `errors` and the optional `nameInputRef`, `onDelete` and `onExpression` are ignored in the dialog; NodeEditDialog passes `errors={[]}` only to satisfy the type and renders its own `NodeIdentity`. ExpressionField's `hideInput` (tested twice) removes the toolbar and inline editor, and with them the only use of `helperText`. Correction to the finder: neither hideInput caller passes `helperText` today, so that combination is reachable only through the type; ConditionFields uses the default "Open in Editor" label, and only TransformFields passes `buttonLabel`.
- **Trigger → consequence:** No wrong behavior today. The design runs against frontend/AGENTS.md:12 ("Prefer explicit variants or composition when independent boolean props produce invalid combinations") and arc-react step 3: every new sidebar-only prop must be optional or faked by the dialog, and `hideInput` plus `helperText` type-checks but renders nothing.
- **Evidence:** Inspector.tsx:44 `presentation?: "sidebar" | "dialog";`, :52 `errors: string[];`. The props are used only inside `{presentation === "sidebar" && (` (:90-123: :95 nameInputRef, :99 `<InspectorProblems key={node.id} errors={errors} />`, :100 onExpression, :112 onDelete). NodeEditDialog.tsx:90-96 renders its own `<NodeIdentity …/>`, with :113 `presentation="dialog"` and :120 `errors={[]}`. ExpressionField.tsx:34 and :39 `{!hideInput && (`, :55 `helperText={helperText}` only inside the hidden branch. Callers: ConditionFields.tsx:189-196 (`hideInput`), TransformFields.tsx:96-99 (`hideInput buttonLabel="Edit as one expression"`).
- **Suggested fix:** Replace both flags with composition. (1) Extract `NodeForm` (new `features/editor/inspector/NodeForm.tsx`): the scroll container with its scroll-to-top effect, `useNodeVariables`, `UnusedProperties`, the registry `Fields` and `ResultFields` (Inspector.tsx:69-87 and :124-134), with props `{ rule, node, rules, readOnly, onNodeChange, onDefinitionChange, onInvalidDefault, onOpenReference }`. `Inspector` becomes the sidebar only: `<aside className="inspector inspector-sidebar">` with the heading (NodeIdentity, InspectorProblems, node-code and Delete buttons), `<NodeForm/>` and the footer, and `errors`, `nameInputRef`, `onDelete` and `onExpression` become required. NodeEditDialog renders `<aside className="inspector inspector-dialog"><NodeForm …/></aside>`, with no `presentation` and no `errors={[]}`. (2) Extract `ExpressionDialogButton` in features/expressions/: the button, its `open` state and the lazy `ExpressionDialog` inside `LazyBoundary` (ExpressionField.tsx:59-85), with `label`, `buttonLabel`, `value`, `variables`, `disabled` and `onChange`. ExpressionField keeps the toolbar and inline editor and composes the button; ConditionFields.tsx:189-196 and TransformFields.tsx:96-99 render `<div className="expression-input"><ExpressionDialogButton …/></div>`, so the DOM and class names (`inspector-dialog`, `.expression-input > .MuiButton-root`) stay the same. Remove `hideInput`. If X201 lands first, `rules` is already gone from these props. No behavior change.
- **Regression test:** Keep `frontend/tests/node-editor.spec.ts` (dialog), `frontend/tests/inspector-header.spec.ts` (sidebar header and delete slot), `frontend/tests/graph-authoring.spec.ts:286`/`:300` ("Edit as one expression · Transform entire value") and `frontend/tests/condition-builder.spec.ts` green; confirm identical styling with the opt-in computed-style comparison in `frontend/tests/style-equivalence.spec.ts` (docs/maintaining.md, Styles).
- **Related:** X201 (removes the `rules` prop from the same props); frontend/AGENTS.md:12; no review lesson covers it.

### X184 · low · cleanup · Browser specs have no shared API fixture helper (28 local create/createRule copies)
- **Status:** CONFIRMED — record: `verdicts/v-clean-fe3.md`
- **Area / module / feature:** Frontend / tests (Playwright browser specs, tests/helpers) / test fixtures that create and publish rules
- **Location:** `frontend/tests/studio-reuse.spec.ts:20` (+ `frontend/tests/studio-refactor.spec.ts:20`, `frontend/tests/helpers/editor.ts:1`)
- **Problem:** Browser specs have no shared API fixture helper. 28 spec files define their own `create`/`createRule`, one pair is byte-identical, and the copies differ in assertions, return values and ID schemes. `tests/helpers` holds only `editor.ts`.
- **Trigger → consequence:** A change to the create or publish payload or status means editing up to 77 `POST /api/rules` calls across 60 spec files and 32 publish calls. The copies already differ: `toBeTruthy()` vs `toBe(true)`, with or without the response text as the message, and Math.random suffixes in 23 files vs Date.now only in the rest. This is test code only; there is no product behavior impact.
- **Evidence:** `grep -l -E '^(async )?function (create|createRule)\b' *.spec.ts | wc -l` → 28. `grep -c 'request.post("/api/rules"'` → 77 calls in 60 files; publish-path calls → 32; Math.random → 23 files. `diff <(sed -n 1,40p studio-reuse.spec.ts) <(sed -n 1,40p studio-refactor.spec.ts)` differs only at line 2 (the import) and line 39 (the test title), so the `definition` fixture and `async function createRule(request, id, name, publish = false)` in lines 4-37 are identical. Assertion variants: 19× `expect(created.ok()).toBeTruthy()`, 17× `expect(response.ok()).toBeTruthy()`, 7× `expect(response.ok(), await response.text()).toBeTruthy()`, 6× with `created.text()`, 5× `.toBe(true)`.
- **Suggested fix:** Add `frontend/tests/helpers/api.ts` with typed helpers on `APIRequestContext`: `uniqueId(prefix)` (Date.now plus a random suffix, capped at 80 characters as the slug policy requires), `createRule(request, { id?, name?, kind = "FORMULA", definition? }): Promise<Rule>` asserting `expect(response.ok(), await response.text()).toBe(true)`, `publishRule(request, rule): Promise<Rule>`, `createSource(request, …)`, `deleteRule(request, id)` for cleanup, and shared minimal definitions (Input → Output). Migrate specs in reviewable batches: first the identical studio-reuse/studio-refactor pair, then specs with the same shape (editor-commands.spec.ts:38-45, editor-resilience.spec.ts:27-38, execution-json-editor.spec.ts:36-48, rule-delete.spec.ts:9-19, node-kind-presentation.spec.ts:17-33), then the rest. Only fixture setup moves; keep every assertion about product behavior.
- **Regression test:** The migrated specs themselves, run against a disposable stack with `ARC_WEB_URL`/`ARC_API_URL` set (AGENTS.md verification rules); no new product assertions.
- **Related:** X21 (tests are never type-checked; a typed helper module pays off once they are); verification lesson "Use unique IDs and display names for fixtures selected by label" (docs/review-lessons.md:120); F30 (studio-reuse.spec.ts and studio-refactor.spec.ts are its regression tests).

### X176 · low · convention · Three nested ternaries remain despite frontend/AGENTS.md and the previous review's claim
- **Status:** CONFIRMED — record: `verdicts/v-clean-fe3.md`
- **Area / module / feature:** Frontend / features/sources/SourcesPage.tsx, features/editor/ReferenceDialog.tsx, features/studio/useArcLanguageSupport.ts / source version chip, referenced-rule viewer navigation, function hover help
- **Location:** `frontend/src/features/sources/SourcesPage.tsx:133` (+ `frontend/src/features/editor/ReferenceDialog.tsx:107`, `frontend/src/features/studio/useArcLanguageSupport.ts:247`, `docs/reviews/2026-09-27-full-review.md:202`)
- **Problem:** Three nested ternaries remain: in SourcesPage's source chip label (inside a JSX prop), ReferenceDialog's navigate handler and useArcLanguageSupport's hover provider. frontend/AGENTS.md:20 says "Extract substantial decisions from JSX; avoid nested ternaries", and docs/reviews/2026-09-27-full-review.md:202 claims "Every remaining nested JSX ternary and several casts are gone". All three predate that review (blame: 1bfe348d, 8389476f, f5ee56af).
- **Trigger → consequence:** Readability only; no wrong behavior. The documented rule and the review's claim do not hold for the code.
- **Evidence:** SourcesPage.tsx:132-136 `` label={ selected.version ? `v${selected.version}${dirty ? " · edited" : ""}` : "Unsaved" } ``. ReferenceDialog.tsx:104-110 `i === s.length - 1 ? { ...entry, mode: route.startsWith("/studio/") ? "code" : "graph", … } : entry`. useArcLanguageSupport.ts:244-258 `return f ? { contents: [ ...(!explicitFunction ? [ … ] : []), … ] } : null;`. A TypeScript-AST scan of frontend/src for a ConditionalExpression inside another's branch, not counting nested JSX elements or functions, reports exactly these three (ReferenceDialog.tsx:107, SourcesPage.tsx:134, useArcLanguageSupport.ts:247); scan scripts: $SP/probes/v-clean-fe3/nested-ternary.cjs and $SP/probes/v-clean-fe3/nested-ternary-nojsx.cjs.
- **Suggested fix:** (1) SourcesPage: a named pure `sourceVersionLabel(version, dirty)` (module-level, or in sourceDocument.ts): ``if (!version) return "Unsaved"; return dirty ? `v${version} · edited` : `v${version}`;``, used by the Chip. (2) ReferenceDialog: replace the handler with X52's `parseRoute` plus `withRoute(entry, route)` and update only the last stack entry (`setStack((s) => [...s.slice(0, -1), withRoute(s[s.length - 1], route)])`). (3) useArcLanguageSupport: `if (!f) return null;`, then `const prefixHint = explicitFunction ? [] : [{ value: … }];` and `return { contents: [...prefixHint, signature, description] };`. Leave the dated review report unchanged, because it is evidence; record the correction in the next review report. No behavior change.
- **Regression test:** New `frontend/tests/unit/code-conventions.spec.ts` that runs the AST scan from $SP/probes/v-clean-fe3/nested-ternary.cjs with the existing `typescript` devDependency over `frontend/src` and expects no ConditionalExpression nested in another's branch (function bodies reset the context, as in the probe).
- **Related:** X52 (the ReferenceDialog instance lives in its private route parser); maintainability row "Prefer typed records, named operations and short loops over positional arrays, nested ternaries or dense streams" (docs/review-lessons.md:100); blame 1bfe348d, 8389476f, f5ee56af.

### X201 · low · altitude · The library tab's current page is threaded into the editor and playground as a name cache and a refresh signal
- **Status:** CONFIRMED — record: `verdicts/v-clean-fe4.md`
- **Area / module / feature:** Frontend / App.tsx, features/editor (Editor, inspector/Inspector, inspector/ReferenceFields, NodeEditDialog, ReferenceDialog), features/execution (ApiPage, usePublishedExecution) / referenced-rule name in the Reference inspector, reference viewer breadcrumb, API playground catalog refresh
- **Location:** `frontend/src/App.tsx:112` (+ `frontend/src/App.tsx:151`, `frontend/src/app/useRuleLibrary.ts:18`, `frontend/src/features/editor/inspector/ReferenceFields.tsx:25`, `frontend/src/features/editor/ReferenceDialog.tsx:58`, `frontend/src/features/execution/usePublishedExecution.ts:54`, `frontend/src/features/execution/usePublishedExecution.ts:69`, `frontend/src/features/editor/inspector/types.ts:7`, `frontend/src/features/editor/types.ts:14`)
- **Problem:** App passes the library tab's current page (`library.rules`) into the editor and ApiPage. That page is keyed by the library's own search/kind/offset and reloads only while the library is visible. The editor threads it through Editor → Inspector → the NodeFieldsProps of all 7 forms (only ReferenceFields reads it), NodeEditDialog, and ReferenceDialog → nested Editor. It acts as a name cache that suppresses the authoritative read, and in ApiPage as a refresh signal. docs/maintaining.md:30 allows other views to use the items "only as hints"; it does not cover a hint that suppresses the fresh read.
- **Trigger → consequence:** (a) The library is searched so child rule C is on its page. C is then renamed by another client, or in this client (acknowledgeSave → library.markChanged only marks the page stale and does not reload it while hidden). Open parent P (#/rules/P?node=ref) without passing through the library: the "Published rule" field shows C's OLD name with 0 GETs of /api/rules/C, and the Reference viewer breadcrumb shows "Old child … v1". Now search the library for something else and reopen P: the field GETs C and shows the NEW name, and the breadcrumb shows the raw rule ID even though the dialog has just loaded C. (b) A direct load of #/playground or #/docs requests the published catalog twice: `libraryReleases` goes from [] to the library page and is part of the catalog's refresh key. The library only changes while ApiPage is mounted on such an initial or in-flight load, so the signal mostly produces extra reads.
- **Evidence:** App.tsx:112 `rules={library.rules}`, :151 `<ApiPage mode={view.page} rules={library.rules} …/>`. useRuleLibrary.ts:18-19 `usePagedResource(JSON.stringify([query, kind]), …)` and :29 `if (!visible || !stale) return;`. ReferenceFields.tsx:25-34 `: rules.find((rule) => rule.id === node.ruleId); … !!node.ruleId && !knownRule`. ReferenceDialog.tsx:58 `{rules.find((r) => r.id === s.ruleId)?.name || s.ruleId}`. usePublishedExecution.ts:54-58 `libraryReleases = rules.map(…)` and :69 `refresh: JSON.stringify([libraryReleases, retry])`. Browser probe on :3191 (library searched for the child, child renamed via PUT): `FIELD (library filtered to child): Old child … child GETs: 0`, `BREADCRUMB: Old child … v1`; after searching the library for something else: `FIELD (library filtered elsewhere): New child … child GETs: 1`, `BREADCRUMB: rv-v-clean-fe4-…-b-child v1`. Direct /#/playground load: `?…&publishedOnly=true`, `?…&kind=`, `?…&publishedOnly=true` (publishedOnly count 2, library 1).
- **Suggested fix:** Each consumer reads what it shows, and `rules` leaves `EditorProps` (features/editor/types.ts:14), Inspector's props, `NodeFieldsProps` (inspector/types.ts:7), NodeEditDialog, ReferenceDialog, ApiPage and `usePublishedExecution(rules, …)`. (1) ReferenceFields.tsx:25-35: drop the `rules.find` shortcut; keep `chosenRule` (the picker's own fresh summary) and otherwise read `ruleApi.get(ruleId)` keyed by the rule ID, the fallback it already has. To avoid a full-rule read on every Reference selection, share one in-flight or bounded summary read per rule ID (see X221). (2) ReferenceDialog: record the loaded rule's name on its stack entry when `loaded.id === current.ruleId` (add `name?: string` to `ReferenceTarget`, or keep a `Map<ruleId, name>` in dialog state) and render the breadcrumb from it, falling back to the ID only before the first load. (3) usePublishedExecution: delete `libraryReleases` (:54-58) from the catalog refresh key (:69) and the `rules.find` term from `listedVersion` (:81-84); the playground relies on its own catalog page and version history (page 0 holds the newest release). ApiPage remounts on navigation, so a publish made elsewhere is still seen on the next visit. Update docs/maintaining.md:30 ("Other views receive the library's current items only as hints…"), since no other view receives them any more. Visible change: referenced-rule names are always current and a direct playground load reads the catalog once; note it in docs/studio.md "Behavior changes in this revision" (Library, sources and playground).
- **Regression test:** Browser, in `frontend/tests/reference-picker.spec.ts` (or `frontend/tests/node-editor.spec.ts` for the viewer): search the library so the child is listed, rename the child via `PUT /api/rules/{child}`, open the parent at `#/rules/<parent>?node=<ref>`, and assert that the field and the reference viewer breadcrumb show the new name. `frontend/tests/execution-catalog.spec.ts`: a direct `/#/playground` load issues exactly one `publishedOnly=true` rule-summaries read.
- **Related:** X05 (the same double catalog read, logged separately), X02 (the playground's implicit version also takes the max with the hidden library page), X221 (ReferenceFields reads the full rule whenever the child is not on the library page), X190 (removes the same prop from inspector props); lessons F33 (a view must not depend on another view's filter or page; this extends it from navigation targets to names and refresh signals), F5 (playground history key), F14 (a save marks the hidden library stale).

<a id="x202"></a>

### X202 · low · altitude · Graph commands choose the selection themselves; Add node half-applies when an invalid default blocks selection
- **Status:** CONFIRMED — record: `verdicts/v-clean-fe4.md`
- **Area / module / feature:** Frontend / features/editor/canvas (useGraphCommands.ts, GraphCanvas.tsx), features/editor (Editor.tsx, nodeSelection.ts) / node selection after Delete node and Add node
- **Location:** `frontend/src/features/editor/canvas/useGraphCommands.ts:56` (+ `frontend/src/features/editor/canvas/useGraphCommands.ts:47`, `frontend/src/features/editor/nodeSelection.ts:6`, `frontend/src/features/editor/Editor.tsx:111`, `frontend/src/features/editor/useRuleDocument.ts:201`)
- **Problem:** Graph commands choose the selection themselves, outside the selection owner, which causes three low-severity behaviours. (1) `removeNode` always selects the Input after an accepted deletion, even when the deleted node was not the selected one; edge deletion, by contrast, clears only a selected edge. (2) This is a second fallback policy (Input first) beside `nodeSelection.defaultSelection` (first Condition), which a code build or version load uses when it removes the selected node. (3) `addNode` runs `edit` before `selectNode`'s invalid-default guard, so the add applies and only the selection is refused.
- **Trigger → consequence:** (1) Select "Result", right-click the unrelated "Spare" and choose Delete: the inspector jumps to "Inputs" although "Result" still exists. (2) In a draft with a Condition, deleting the selected node selects the Input, while removing the same node through a code build selects the first Condition. (3) Type an ARRAY default `[` in the Input form, then Add node → Formula: the node count goes 2 → 3, the alert says "Fix the invalid parameter default before selecting another node", and the selection stays on Inputs. No data is lost (the buffer "[" is kept), but the command half-applies with a message about selection, so a user may add again and stack nodes at the same screen-centre position.
- **Evidence:** useGraphCommands.ts:56-60 `const next = definition.nodes.find((node) => node.type === "INPUT") || definition.nodes.find((node) => node.id !== id); if (edit((current) => removeGraphNode(current, id))) selectNode(next?.id || "");` and :47-51 `const added = edit(…); if (added) selectNode(id);`. nodeSelection.ts:6 `definition.nodes.find((node) => node.type === "CONDITION") ?? definition.nodes[0]`. Editor.tsx:111 `if (id !== selected && blockedByInvalidDefault(action)) return false;`. useRuleDocument.ts:201-208: `edit` does not check invalid defaults. GraphCanvas.tsx removeEdge: `if (removed && selectedEdge === id) setSelectedEdge(null);`. Right-click does not select (onNodeContextMenu only opens the menu). Browser probe: `SELECTED AFTER DELETING UNSELECTED NODE: Inputs`; `NODES 2 -> 3 ALERTS ["Fix the invalid parameter default before selecting another node"] SELECTED Inputs DEFAULT BUFFER [`. No doc or test pins selection after deleting an unrelated node.
- **Suggested fix:** Keep the selection policy in one owner: a `useNodeSelection` hook beside `features/editor/nodeSelection.ts` holding `requestedSelection`, the invalid-default guard (`mayChangeSelection(action)`, wrapping `blockedByInvalidDefault`) and the derived `selectedNode(draft, requested)` fallback; Editor.tsx:103-117 moves into it. Then: (1) `removeNode` (useGraphCommands.ts:54-61) only edits, `if (canRemoveGraphNode(definition, id)) edit((current) => removeGraphNode(current, id))`; an unrelated deletion keeps the selection, and deleting the selected node falls back through `defaultSelection`, the same policy as builds and version loads. (2) `addNode` checks `mayChangeSelection("adding a node")` before `edit` and selects the new node only after an accepted edit, so the command is refused whole with `invalidDefaultMessage("adding a node")` ("Fix the invalid parameter default before adding a node"). (3) Derive the selected edge from the draft the same way (null when its edge is gone), which also fixes X98. Decision needed: the fallback after deleting the selected node. Recommend `defaultSelection` everywhere, which docs/studio.md already describes ("The selection falls back to the default node when its node disappears"); if Input-first is preferred, change `defaultSelection` itself so builds and version loads agree. Note the visible change in docs/studio.md "Behavior changes in this revision" (Editor).
- **Regression test:** `frontend/tests/node-context-menu.spec.ts`: with "Result" selected, context-menu Delete on an unrelated node keeps the inspector on "Result"; deleting the selected node selects the default node. `frontend/tests/editor-buffer-guards.spec.ts`: with an ARRAY default "[" pending, Add node → Formula leaves the node count at 2, shows the invalid-default message and keeps the buffer. `frontend/tests/unit/document.spec.ts` (selection fallback): `selectedNode` returns `defaultSelection` after the requested node is removed.
- **Related:** X99 (the same unrelated-deletion jump, logged from `verdicts/v-editor2.md`; one fix covers both), X98 (a stale "Delete connection" after the edge is removed with its node); lessons F5 ("Derive the selected node from the current draft, falling back to the default selection"), F6 (invalid-default guards on transitions); maintainability row on the command gate (docs/review-lessons.md:108; "callers move the selection … only after an accepted edit").
